Proposition <a href="#prop:atomic-intermediate" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="prop:atomic-intermediate">47</a> uses exactly these packets at every intermediate index; its error bound <a href="#eq:atomic-prefix-error" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="eq:atomic-prefix-error">[eq:atomic-prefix-error]</a> is in expectation before the last array is forgotten. Its differential inequality holds globally after cancellation of the nuclear singularity, its lower barrier persists at every index, and its exterior equality holds beyond $L_1r_j$. The construction may be stopped at any prefix without changing the master smoothing.

<a id="pm-h-66"></a>
###### Classical comparison and the neutral offset.

The Thomas–Fermi minimizer and point-source comparison required for coefficient identification are Lemmas <a href="#m:lem:barrier-global-tf" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="m:lem:barrier-global-tf">31</a> and <a href="#m:lem:barrier-mixture" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="m:lem:barrier-mixture">32</a>, valid for every positive real point charge. The quantum offset needed for neutral states is Proposition <a href="#a:prop:tail-offset" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="a:prop:tail-offset">50</a>. It follows from zero-offset deletion and is uniform over all neutral ground states by sector monotonicity. These are independent inputs: the excess-charge conclusion itself does not replace either the Thomas–Fermi estimates or the neutral-sector argument.

  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "Radial supports in the interior comparison (schematic, not to scale). Fine smears of retained particles lie inside the TF domain, and the upper packets remain at distance at least $3b$ from the conditional core. This particle-support geometry is common to both applications. The atomic distance scale makes $B(y,20a)$ free of nuclei; the molecular scale may leave nuclei there, and their packet loss is retained in [m:eq:screen-comparison].",
      "figure_id": "m:fig:screen-patch",
      "pdf_page": 23,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "m:fig:screen-patch",
          "exact_quote": "Radial supports in the interior comparison (schematic, not to\n scale). Fine smears of retained particles lie inside the TF domain,\n and the upper packets remain at distance at least $3b$ from the\n conditional core. This particle-support geo",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=.88cm,y=.86cm,font=\\small]\n  \\fill[black!4] (9,-.45) rectangle (11,4.65);\n  \\draw[densely dashed,black!55] (9,-.45)--(9,4.65);\n  \\node[align=center,font=\\footnotesize] at (10,4.95) {core may\\\\occur here};\n  \\foreach \\yy/\\endx/\\lefttext/\\righttext in {\n       4/4.5/{retained centers}/{$t_0-7b$},\n       3/5.65/{fine density $\\sigma$}/{$t_0-(7-\\sqrt3)b$},\n       2/6.7/{TF centers $\\rho$}/{$t_0-4b$},\n       1/7.45/{upper packets}/{$t_0-3b$},\n       0/9/{full-out region}/{$t_0$}} {\n    \\node[anchor=east] at (-.22,\\yy) {\\lefttext};\n    \\draw[very thick,blue!55!black] (0,\\yy)--(\\endx,\\yy);\n    \\draw[blue!55!black] (0,\\yy-.1)--(0,\\yy+.1);\n    \\draw[blue!55!black] (\\endx,\\yy-.1)--(\\endx,\\yy+.1);\n    \\node[anchor=south west,font=\\footnotesize,inner sep=2pt]\n      at (\\endx,\\yy+.09) {\\righttext};\n  }\n  \\node[anchor=north,font=\\footnotesize] at (0,-.45) {$|x-y|=0$};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Schematic diagram showing radial support intervals along horizontal lines originating from $|x - y| = 0$ on the left. A vertical shaded region on the right is labeled \"core may occur here\" starting at $t_0$. Five horizontal lines are labeled from top to bottom:\n- \"retained centers\" with a horizontal interval extending from $|x - y| = 0$ to a tick mark at $t_0 - 7b$.\n- \"fine density $\\sigma$\" with a horizontal interval extending from $|x - y| = 0$ to a tick mark at $t_0 - (7 - \\sqrt{3})b$.\n- \"TF centers $\\rho$\" with a horizontal interval extending from $|x - y| = 0$ to a tick mark at $t_0 - 4b$.\n- \"upper packets\" with a horizontal interval extending from $|x - y| = 0$ to a tick mark at $t_0 - 3b$.\n- \"full-out region\" with a horizontal interval starting at a tick mark at $t_0$ and extending to the right into the shaded region.\nA vertical line marks $|x - y| = 0$ on the left."
    }
  ],
